### [1] [How Professional Visual Artists are Negotiating Generative AI in the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04537)
*Harry H. Jiang,Jordan Taylor,William Agnew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对378名专业视觉艺术家的调查显示，大多数艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，通过多种拒绝策略应对职场压力，并报告了AI带来的负面职业影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在流行媒体和HCI研究中受到艺术家批评，但缺乏对专业艺术家职场和职业影响的理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，了解生成式AI如何影响专业视觉艺术家的职业生涯和工作环境。

Method: 对378名经过验证的专业视觉艺术家进行问卷调查，研究生成式AI（文本或视觉）对其职业生涯和工作场所的影响。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 大多数视觉艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，并通过多种拒绝策略应对职场中的AI应用；(2) 艺术家环境中存在多种因素影响其使用生成式AI，包括来自客户、老板和同行的压力；(3) 视觉艺术家普遍报告生成式AI对其工作场所产生负面影响，导致压力增加和工作机会减少。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，鼓励HCI研究人员更深入地考虑艺术家在职场中不愿使用生成式AI的意愿，并关注AI技术对艺术职业生态的实际影响。

Abstract: Generative AI has been heavily critiqued by artists in both popular media and HCI scholarship. However, more work is needed to understand the impacts of generative AI on professional artists' workplaces and careers. In this paper, we conduct a survey of \textit{378 verified professional visual artists} about how generative AI has impacted their careers and workplaces. We find (1) most visual artists are strongly opposed to using generative AI (text or visual) and negotiate their inclusion in the workplace through a variety of \textit{refusal} strategies (2) there exist a range of factors in artists environments shaping their use of generative AI, including pressure from clients, bosses, and peers and (3) visual artists report overwhelmingly negative impacts of generative AI on their workplaces, leading to added stress and reduced job opportunities. In light of these findings, we encourage HCI researchers to contend more deeply with artists' desires not to use generative AI in the workplace.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond the Interface: Redefining UX for Society-in-the-Loop AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04552)
*Nahal Mafi,Sahar Maleki,Babak Rahimi Ardabili,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的AI人机协同系统用户体验评估框架，超越传统前端可用性，涵盖后端性能、组织工作流和决策结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户体验框架针对确定性系统设计，无法捕捉AI人机协同系统中概率性输出和人类参与带来的社会技术动态变化，需要新的评估方法。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对269个利益相关者见解进行归纳性社会建构分析；2）部署一个操作性的HITL视频异常检测系统进行实证研究。

Result: 研究发现利益相关者通过风险、治理和组织能力等多维度主题体验AI；实验结果显示检测行为和警报路由直接影响人类监督和工作量。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，提出了一个以四个社会技术指标为中心的新评估框架：准确性、操作延迟、适应时间和信任度，将用户体验重新定义为跨越基础设施和治理的多层结构。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly operate in decision-critical environments where probabilistic outputs and Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) interactions reshape user engagement. Traditional user experience (UX) frameworks, designed for deterministic systems, fail to capture these evolving sociotechnical dynamics. This paper argues that in AI-enabled HITL systems, UX must transcend frontend usability to encompass backend performance, organizational workflows, and decision making structures.
  We employ a mixed-methods approach, combining an inductive social construction analysis of 269 stakeholder insights with the deployment of an operational HITL video anomaly detection system. Our findings reveal that stakeholders experience AI through multifaceted themes: risk, governance, and organizational capacity. Experimental results further demonstrate how detection behavior and alert routing directly calibrate human oversight and workload. Grounded in these results, we formalize a new evaluative framework centered on four sociotechnical metrics: Accuracy (FPR/FNR), Operational Latency (response time), Adaptation Time (deployment burden), and Trust (validated automation scales). This framework redefines UX as a multi-layered construct spanning infrastructure and governance, providing a rigorous foundation for evaluating AI systems embedded within complex real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: a Spectrum of Interface Metaphors for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04613)
*Jianna So,Connie Cheng,Sonia Krishna Murthy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出重新定位拟人化作为设计变量，开发从"反拟人化"到"超拟人化"的隐喻光谱，以揭示LLMs作为社会技术系统的本质，促进批判性参与而非优化可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs界面过度强化拟人化隐喻，用户常将其视为真人，导致错觉和伤害。拟人化界面强调LLMs与人类的相似性，却掩盖了关键差异，且缺乏安全措施。

Method: 将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，开发从透明度驱动的"反拟人化"到怪异的"超拟人化"的隐喻光谱。这些隐喻为界面隐喻引入物质性，揭示LLMs作为由人类劳动、基础设施和数据塑造的社会技术系统。

Result: 提出一个理论框架，展示界面隐喻如何塑造和颠覆默认隐喻。该光谱将界面设计从优化可用性转向鼓励批判性参与，帮助用户理解LLMs的真实本质。

Conclusion: 通过将拟人化作为设计变量并开发隐喻光谱，可以打破默认的拟人化隐喻，促进用户对LLMs作为社会技术系统的批判性理解，减少错觉和潜在伤害。

Abstract: Anthropomorphizing conversational technology is a natural human tendency. Today, the anthropomorphic metaphor is overly reinforced across intelligent tools. Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly anthropomorphized through interface design. While metaphors are inherently partial, anthropomorphic interfaces highlight similarities between LLMs and humans, but mask crucial differences. As a result, the metaphor is often taken literally; users treat LLMs as if they are truly human. With few safeguards in place, this extreme anthropomorphism drives users to delusion and harm. Users also experience dissonance between the ethics of using LLMs, their growing ubiquity, and limited interface alternatives. We propose repositioning anthropomorphism as a design variable, developing opposing extremes as a theoretical framework for how interface metaphors shape and can disrupt the default metaphor. We introduce a spectrum of metaphors from transparency-driven ''anti-anthropomorphism'' to uncanny ''hyper-anthropomorphism''. These metaphors introduce materiality to interface metaphors, exposing LLMs as sociotechnical systems shaped by human labor, infrastructure, and data. This spectrum shifts interface design away from optimizing usability and toward encouraging critical engagement.

</details>


### [4] [Gamified Informed Decision-Making for Performance-Aware Design by Non-Experts: An Exoskeleton Design Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04643)
*Arman Khalilbeigi Khameneh,Armin Mostafavi,Alicia Nahmad Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个游戏化的决策支持框架，将游戏引擎与实时性能反馈结合，帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间，应用于外骨骼幕墙改造设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持系统对非专业设计师探索复杂性能驱动设计空间至关重要。现有工具往往缺乏实时反馈和用户友好性，需要开发更直观、互动性强的框架来提升设计决策质量。

Method: 开发游戏化决策框架，集成游戏引擎提供实时性能反馈。性能标准包括结构行为、环境参数、制造、材料和成本考量。通过24名建筑学生和非专业设计师参与外骨骼幕墙改造设计实验，评估框架效果。

Result: 参与者能够迭代修改幕墙几何形状，同时接收三大关键标准的实时反馈：1)结构行为(挠度、质量、应力/强度比)；2)环境参数(太阳得热、供暖/制冷能耗)；3)制造考量(制造成本、材料成本、机器人加工、材料设置)。游戏化反馈机制显著增强用户理解和知情决策能力。

Conclusion: 游戏化环境通过知情决策和性能标准的定制化协商，使利益相关者能够参与设计过程。精心策划的设计空间和结构化指导相比开放式生成工具提高了效率，为预占用评估做出贡献。

Abstract: Decision Support Systems (DSS) play a crucial role in enabling non-expert designers to explore complex, performance-driven design spaces. This paper presents a gamified decision-making framework that integrates game engines with real-time performance feedback. Performance criteria include structural behavior, environmental parameters, fabrication, material, and cost considerations. The developed design framework was tested with architecture students and non-expert designers on the design of an exoskeleton facade to retrofit an existing building. Participants (N=24) were able to iteratively modify façade geometries while receiving real-time feedback across the three key criteria: 1) structural behavior, including deflection, mass, and stress/strength ratio; 2) environmental parameters, such as solar gain and heating/cooling energy demands; and 3) fabrication considerations, including fabrication and material costs, robotic machining, and material setup. The evaluation of participant interactions reveals that gamified feedback mechanisms significantly enhance user comprehension and informed decision-making across the criteria. Further, participants' understanding of structural, material, and fabrication performance in relation to the iterative design task suggests that curated design spaces and structured guidance improve efficiency compared to open-ended generative tools. This research contributes to pre-occupancy evaluations, demonstrating how gamified environments enable stakeholder participation in the design process through informed decisionmaking and customized negotiation of performance criteria. .

</details>


### [5] [VizCrit: Exploring Strategies for Displaying Computational Feedback in a Visual Design Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04754)
*Mingyi Li,Mengyi Chen,Sarah Luo,Yining Cao,Haijun Xia,Maitraye Das,Steven P. Dow,Jane L. E*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizCrit系统通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成提供可操作性反馈，研究发现解决方案导向的反馈比教科书式反馈能减少更多设计问题并提升自我感知创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计教学中教师通常提供多模态反馈（标注+文本），但反馈的"可操作性"存在差异。研究旨在探索如何通过创意工具实现支持可操作性谱系的标注反馈，以及反馈可操作性如何影响新手的设计过程行为、创造力感知、设计原则学习和整体成果。

Method: 开发VizCrit系统，通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成提供计算反馈，支持可操作性谱系。进行组间实验(N=36)，新手在三种条件下修改设计：教科书式反馈、意识导向反馈、解决方案导向反馈。

Result: 解决方案导向反馈比教科书式反馈导致更少的设计问题和更高的自我感知创造力，但专家对创造力的评分无显著差异。意识导向反馈在减少设计问题方面也优于教科书式反馈。

Conclusion: AI创意支持工具应校准反馈可操作性，帮助新手在生产力与学习、成长、设计意识发展之间取得平衡。解决方案导向反馈能提升效率和自我效能感，但需注意对创造性发展的潜在影响。

Abstract: Visual design instructors often provide multi-modal feedback, mixing annotations with text. Prior theory emphasizes the importance of actionable feedback, where "actionability" lies on a spectrum--from surfacing relevant design concepts to suggesting concrete fixes. How might creativity tools implement annotations that support such feedback, and how does the actionability of feedback impact novices' process-related behaviors, perceptions of creativity, learning of design principles, and overall outcomes? We introduce VizCrit, a system for providing computational feedback that supports the actionability spectrum, realized through algorithmic issue detection and visual annotation generation. In a between-subjects study (N=36), novices revised a design under one of three conditions: textbook-based, awareness-centered, or solution-centered feedback. We found that solution-centered feedback led to fewer design issues and higher self-perceived creativity compared with textbook-based feedback, although expert ratings on creativity showed no significant differences. We discuss the implications for AI in Creativity Support Tools, including the potential of calibrating feedback actionability to help novices balance productivity with learning, growth, and developing design awareness.

</details>


### [6] [Can LLMs Synthesize Court-Ready Statistical Evidence? Evaluating AI-Assisted Sentencing Bias Analysis for California Racial Justice Act Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04804)
*Aparna Komarla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Redo.io是一个开源平台，通过处理加州监狱记录生成种族偏见量刑的法庭证据，并探索LLM辅助解释统计结果的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加州通过了《种族正义法案》(2020)，允许被告基于种族差异统计证据挑战定罪，但政策实施滞后于立法意图，导致数百个重新量刑机会未被识别，形成"第二次机会差距"。

Method: 开发Redo.io开源平台，处理通过《加州公共记录法案》获取的95,000份监狱记录；使用比值比、相对风险、卡方检验等统计方法生成法庭证据；探索LLM驱动的解释层，将统计结果合成为包含置信区间、样本量和数据限制的连贯叙述；使用LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估LLM与统计学家的表现。

Result: 平台能够生成法庭就绪的种族偏见量刑统计证据；评估表明AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在分析流程中道德地整合时，能够实时生成证据。

Conclusion: AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在分析流程中道德地整合时，能够实时生成种族偏见证据，帮助弥合加州重新量刑中的"第二次机会差距"。

Abstract: Resentencing in California remains a complex legal challenge despite legislative reforms like the Racial Justice Act (2020), which allows defendants to challenge convictions based on statistical evidence of racial disparities in sentencing and charging. Policy implementation lags behind legislative intent, creating a 'second-chance gap' where hundreds of resentencing opportunities remain unidentified. We present Redo.io, an open-source platform that processes 95,000 prison records acquired under the California Public Records Act (CPRA) and generates court-ready statistical evidence of racial bias in sentencing for prima facie and discovery motions. We explore the design of an LLM-powered interpretive layer that synthesizes results from statistical methods like Odds Ratio, Relative Risk, and Chi-Square Tests into cohesive narratives contextualized with confidence intervals, sample sizes, and data limitations. Our evaluations comparing LLM performance to statisticians using the LLM-as-a-Judge framework suggest that AI can serve as a powerful descriptive assistant for real-time evidence generation when ethically incorporated in the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [7] [SparkTales: Facilitating Cross-Language Collaborative Storytelling through Coordinator-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04806)
*Wenxin Zhao,Peng Zhang,Hansu Gu,Haoxuan Zhou,Xiaojie Huo,Lin Wang,Wen Zheng,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SparkTales是一个智能支持系统，旨在减轻跨语言协作故事讲述中协调员的工作负担，同时提升儿童的参与度和互动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言协作故事讲述对儿童语言学习和文化发展至关重要，但实践中儿童参与度浅，协调员面临认知和组织负担过重的问题，需要协调语言支持、维持儿童参与度和处理文化差异。

Method: 通过形成性研究了解协调员需求和痛点，设计SparkTales智能支持系统，利用参与儿童的个体和共同特征，为协调员提供故事框架、多样化问题和理解导向材料。

Result: SparkTales显著提高了协调员的工作效率和指导质量，同时改善了儿童的参与度，为未来支持跨语言协作的智能系统设计提供了宝贵见解。

Conclusion: SparkTales成功解决了跨语言协作故事讲述中的关键挑战，通过智能支持系统有效减轻协调员负担并提升儿童参与质量，为相关领域系统设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-language collaborative storytelling plays a vital role in children's language learning and cultural development, fostering both expressive ability and intercultural awareness. Yet, in practice, children's participation is often shallow, and facilitating such sessions places heavy cognitive and organizational burdens on coordinators, who must coordinate language support, maintain children's engagement, and navigate cultural differences. To address these challenges, we conducted a formative study with coordinators to identify their needs and pain points, which guided the design of SparkTales, an intelligent support system for cross-language collaborative storytelling. SparkTales leverages both individual and common characteristics of participating children to provide coordinators with story frameworks, diverse questions, and comprehension-oriented materials, aiming to reduce coordinators' workload while enhancing children's interactive engagement. Evaluation results show that SparkTales not only significantly increases coordinators' efficiency and quality of guidance but also improves children's participation, providing valuable insights for the design of future intelligent systems supporting cross-language collaboration.

</details>


### [8] [Roomify: Spatially-Grounded Style Transformation for Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04917)
*Xueyang Wang,Qinxuan Cen,Weitao Bi,Yunxiang Ma,Xin Yi,Robert Xiao,Xinyi Fu,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Roomify是一个空间锚定的转换系统，通过AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，将物理房间转换为主题化虚拟环境，同时保持空间结构和功能语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR方法面临根本性权衡：完全沉浸会牺牲空间感知，而透视解决方案会破坏临场感。需要一种既能保持空间意识又能提供沉浸体验的解决方案。

Method: 采用空间锚定转换方法，将物理空间视为"空间容器"，保持家具的关键功能和几何属性，同时实现彻底的风格变化。结合现场3D场景理解、AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，创建基于物理现实的个性化虚拟环境。开发跨现实创作工具，通过MR编辑和VR预览工作流程实现细粒度用户控制。

Result: 用户研究显示：18名VR用户的临场感比透视方案提高63%，比完全虚拟基准提高26%，同时保持空间感知。8名设计专业人士评估显示场景质量5.95/7，创意支持6.08/7，系统在多样化环境中具有专业工作流程价值。

Conclusion: Roomify通过空间锚定转换解决了VR中沉浸与空间感知的权衡问题，既能提供高临场感，又能保持空间意识，为创意表达和专业工作流程提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present Roomify, a spatially-grounded transformation system that generates themed virtual environments anchored to users' physical rooms while maintaining spatial structure and functional semantics. Current VR approaches face a fundamental trade-off: full immersion sacrifices spatial awareness, while passthrough solutions break presence. Roomify addresses this through spatially-grounded transformation - treating physical spaces as "spatial containers" that preserve key functional and geometric properties of furniture while enabling radical stylistic changes. Our pipeline combines in-situ 3D scene understanding, AI-driven spatial reasoning, and style-aware generation to create personalized virtual environments grounded in physical reality. We introduce a cross-reality authoring tool enabling fine-grained user control through MR editing and VR preview workflows. Two user studies validate our approach: one with 18 VR users demonstrates a 63% improvement in presence over passthrough and 26% over fully virtual baselines while maintaining spatial awareness; another with 8 design professionals confirms the system's creative expressiveness (scene quality: 5.95/7; creativity support: 6.08/7) and professional workflow value across diverse environments.

</details>


### [9] [Mind the Gap: Mapping Wearer-Bystander Privacy Tensions and Context-Adaptive Pathways for Camera Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04930)
*Xueyang Wang,Kewen Peng,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统评估揭示智能眼镜隐私机制中的利益相关者期望差距，提出基于情境的动态隐私保护路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能眼镜佩戴者与旁观者之间的隐私冲突，探索如何平衡记录功能与隐私保护之间的基本矛盾

Method: 采用多利益相关者评估方法，包括中国地区的问卷调查（N=525）和配对访谈（N=20），研究分为两个部分：量化期望-意愿差距和评估12种隐私增强技术

Result: 发现佩戴者与旁观者之间存在显著的期望差距，特别是在敏感情境下65-90%的旁观者会采取防御行动；识别出当前隐私技术的四个基本权衡：可见性与干扰性、赋权与负担、保护与自主性、问责与暴露

Conclusion: 隐私可接受性主要由情境决定，提出情境自适应保护路径：公共场所最小摩擦可见性、半公共环境结构化协商、敏感情境自动保护，为普适感知中的情境感知设计提供诊断框架

Abstract: Camera glasses create fundamental privacy tensions between wearers seeking recording functionality and bystanders concerned about unauthorized surveillance. We present a systematic multi-stakeholder evaluation of privacy mechanisms through surveys (N=525) and paired interviews (N=20) in China. Study 1 quantifies expectation-willingness gaps: bystanders consistently demand stronger information transparency and protective measures than wearers will provide, with disparities intensifying in sensitive contexts where 65-90% of bystanders would take defensive action. Study 2 evaluates twelve privacy-enhancing technologies, revealing four fundamental trade-offs that undermine current approaches: visibility versus disruption, empowerment versus burden, protection versus agency, and accountability versus exposure. These gaps reflect structural incompatibilities rather than inadequate goodwill, with context emerging as the primary determinant of privacy acceptability. We propose context-adaptive pathways that dynamically adjust protection strategies: minimal-friction visibility in public spaces, structured negotiation in semi-public environments, and automatic protection in sensitive contexts. Our findings contribute a diagnostic framework for evaluating privacy mechanisms and implications for context-aware design in ubiquitous sensing.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond Advocacy: A Design Space for Replication-Related Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04959)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于复制研究设计的四维度三层次设计空间框架，支持复制实验的识别、分类、比较和分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究在可视化、HCI及所有科学领域都很重要，但在进行复制研究时需要决定哪些方面保持不变、哪些需要改变，需要系统化的设计决策支持框架

Method: 将复制实验设计视为成对比较问题，构建包含四个实践维度的设计空间框架，每个维度由三个比较层次定义，支持回顾性特征描述和前瞻性规划

Result: 开发了一个多维设计空间框架，提供了工作示例，并将该框架与其他描述复制研究范围的尝试联系起来

Conclusion: 该框架为复制研究的设计决策提供了系统化的支持工具，有助于识别、分类、比较和分析复制实验设计中的各种变化

Abstract: The importance of replication is often discussed and advocated -- not only in the domains of visualization and HCI, but in all scientific areas. When replicating a study, design decisions need to be made with regards which aspects of the original study will remain the same and which will be altered. We present a supporting multi-dimensional design space framework within which such decisions can be identified, categorized, compared and analyzed. The framework treats replication experimental design as a pairwise comparison problem, and represents the design by four practical dimensions defined by three comparison levels. The design space is therefore a framework that can be used for both retrospective characterization and prospective planning. We provide worked examples, and relate our framework to other attempts at describing the scope of replication studies.

</details>


### [11] [Auto-Generating Personas from User Reviews in VR App Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04985)
*Yi Wang,Kexin Cheng,Xiao Liu,Chetan Arora,John Grundy,Thuong Hoang,Henry Been-Lirn Duh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了VR课程中的自动生成人物角色系统，用于促进无障碍需求讨论和VR设计开发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物角色是讨论软件设计中无障碍需求的有价值工具，但在VR项目中用于无障碍需求获取的应用有限且面临挑战，需要填补这一空白

Method: 在VR课程中开发了自动生成人物角色系统，使用这些人物角色来促进无障碍需求讨论并指导VR设计和开发

Result: 自动生成人物角色系统使学生能够更有效地培养同理心，该系统在VR课程环境中成功用于获取潜在的无障碍需求

Conclusion: 自动生成人物角色系统在VR课程环境中可作为获取潜在无障碍需求的有效手段，为VR项目中的无障碍需求讨论提供了实用工具

Abstract: Personas are a valuable tool for discussing accessibility requirements in software design and development practices. However, the use of personas for accessibility-focused requirements elicitation in VR projects remains limited and is accompanied by several challenges. To fill this gap, we developed an auto-generated persona system in a VR course, where the personas were used to facilitate discussions on accessibility requirements and to guide VR design and development. Our findings indicate that the auto-generated persona system enabled students to develop empathy more efficiently. This study demonstrates the use of automatically generated personas in VR course settings as a means of eliciting latent accessibility requirements.

</details>


### [12] [Haptics in Cognition: Disruptor or Enabler of Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05019)
*Bibeg Limbu,Irene-Angelica Chounta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性试点研究调查触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）对通过手写进行信息保留（即时回忆）学习的影响，发现增加书写压力可能轻微损害回忆，而单独使用手套无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）如何影响通过手写进行的学习，特别是信息保留，旨在理解具身交互与认知结果之间的复杂关系，为设计基于传感器的多模态学习环境提供依据。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计的探索性试点研究，20名参与者随机分配到四个实验组，操纵触觉（通过使用手套）和运动（通过增加书写压力）。测量信息保留（即时回忆测试）、心理努力（次要任务反应时间）和感知工作量（NASA-TLX）作为中介变量。使用贝叶斯二项回归和贝叶斯中介分析。

Result: 贝叶斯二项回归显示中等证据表明增加书写压力对回忆有负面影响（85-88%概率为负效应），而单独使用手套无明确效应。贝叶斯中介分析未发现心理努力或感知工作量中介这些效应的强证据，所有95%可信区间包含零，表明存在实质性不确定性。

Conclusion: 增加动觉需求可能轻微损害即时回忆，且独立于感知工作量或心理努力。单独操纵触觉似乎不影响信息保留。研究有助于理解具身交互与认知结果之间的细微关系，对设计传感器多模态学习环境有启示。

Abstract: This exploratory pilot study investigates the impact of haptic perception --specifically tactile sensitivity (touch) and kinaesthetic intensity (movement)-- on learning, operationalized as information retention (immediate recall) through handwriting. Participants (N=20) were randomly assigned to one of four experimental groups in a 2x2 factorial design, manipulating touch (via glove use) and movement (via increased writing pressure). Information retention was measured using an immediate recall test, while mental effort (reaction time in a secondary task) and perceived workload (NASA-TLX) were examined as mediating variables. Bayesian binomial regression revealed moderate evidence that increased writing pressure negatively influenced recall (85-88% probability of negative effect), whereas glove use alone demonstrated no clear effect. Bayesian mediation analysis found no strong evidence that mental effort or perceived workload mediated these effects, as all 95% credible intervals included zero, indicating substantial uncertainty. These findings suggest that increased Kinaesthetic demands may slightly impair immediate recall, independent of perceived workload or mental effort. Importantly, the manipulation of touch alone does not appear to influence information retention. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between embodied interactions and cognitive outcomes, with implications for designing sensor-based multimodal learning environments.

</details>


### [13] [Wire Your Way: Hardware-Contextualized Guidance and In-situ Tests for Personalized Circuit Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05085)
*Punn Lertjaturaphat,Jungwoo Rhee,Jaewon You,Andrea Bianchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个支持个性化电路构建和调试的原型平台，通过增强型面包板提供电路感知、上下文引导和交互测试功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教程和工具包采用逐步指导的方式，但创客在电路构建和调试方面有独特偏好和个性化方法，这种不匹配需要解决

Method: 使用电路感知的增强型面包板，支持通过上下文引导进行实时硬件重新配置，并通过交互测试实现原位电路验证

Result: 可用性研究(N=12)表明，创客能够利用电路感知引导和调试功能来支持个性化的构建模式

Conclusion: 提出的原型平台通过支持个性化电路构建和调试，解决了传统教程方法与创客实际需求之间的不匹配问题

Abstract: The increasing popularity of microcontroller platforms like Arduino enables diverse end-user developers to participate in circuit prototyping. Traditionally, follow-along tutorials serve as an essential learning method for makers, and in fact, several prior toolkits leveraged this format as a way to engage new makers. However, literature and our formative study (N=12) show that makers have unique preferences regarding the construction of their circuits and idiosyncratic ways to assess and debug problems, which contrasts with the step-by-step instructional nature of tutorials and those systems leveraging this method. To address this mismatch, we present a prototyping platform that supports personalized circuit construction and debugging. Our system utilizes an augmented breadboard, which is circuit-aware and supports on-the-fly hardware reconfiguration via contextualized guidance and in-situ circuit validation through interactive tests. Through a usability study (N=12), we demonstrate how makers leverage circuit-aware guidance and debugging to support individual building patterns.

</details>


### [14] [Not All Trust is the Same: Effects of Decision Workflow and Explanations in Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05229)
*Laura Spillner,Rachel Ringe,Robert Porzel,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了AI辅助决策中不同工作流程（一步vs两步）、解释机制以及用户领域知识和AI经验对信任校准的影响，发现两步设置并未减少过度依赖，工作流程与解释机制存在交互效应，且报告信任与依赖行为是不同构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助决策中的核心挑战是实现合理校准的信任，既要防止过度信任（接受错误AI建议），也要防止信任不足（拒绝正确建议）。先前研究在工作流程设计（一步vs两步设置）和信任测量方式（自我报告vs行为信任）上存在差异，需要系统研究这些因素及其交互作用。

Method: 研究采用实验设计，考察三个主要因素：(a)决策工作流程类型（一步设置：用户立即看到AI建议；两步设置：用户先提交初步决策再看到AI建议），(b)解释机制的存在与否，(c)用户的领域知识和先前AI经验。测量指标包括报告信任、依赖行为（同意率和切换率）以及过度依赖。

Result: 结果显示：1）两步设置并未减少过度依赖；2）决策工作流程不直接影响自我报告信任，但与领域知识和解释机制存在交叉交互效应，表明解释机制的效果可能无法跨工作流程泛化；3）报告信任与依赖行为是不同构念，应在AI辅助决策中分别评估。

Conclusion: AI辅助决策中，工作流程设计（一步vs两步）对减少过度依赖效果有限，解释机制的效果受工作流程设置调节。研究强调了区分报告信任与行为信任的重要性，为设计更有效的AI辅助决策系统提供了实证依据。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI-assisted decision making is achieving warranted, well-calibrated trust. Both overtrust (accepting incorrect AI recommendations) and undertrust (rejecting correct advice) should be prevented. Prior studies differ in the design of the decision workflow - whether users see the AI suggestion immediately (1-step setup) or have to submit a first decision beforehand (2-step setup) -, and in how trust is measured - through self-reports or as behavioral trust, that is, reliance. We examined the effects and interactions of (a) the type of decision workflow, (b) the presence of explanations, and (c) users' domain knowledge and prior AI experience. We compared reported trust, reliance (agreement rate and switch rate), and overreliance. Results showed no evidence that a 2-step setup reduces overreliance. The decision workflow also did not directly affect self-reported trust, but there was a crossover interaction effect with domain knowledge and explanations, suggesting that the effects of explanations alone may not generalize across workflow setups. Finally, our findings confirm that reported trust and reliance behavior are distinct constructs that should be evaluated separately in AI-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [15] [AttentiveLearn: Personalized Post-Lecture Support for Gaze-Aware Immersive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05324)
*Shi Liu,Martin Feick,Linus Bierhoff,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AttentiveLearn：基于VR眼动追踪的个性化课后测验系统，通过分析学习者在虚拟课堂中的注意力分布，在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验，提升学习动机和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式学习环境（如VR虚拟教室）为学习者提供独特体验，但有效学习支持仍具挑战。现有HCI研究主要关注课中支持，而课后支持对维持学习动机、参与度和学习成果至关重要，却鲜有研究。

Method: 提出AttentiveLearn学习生态系统：1）通过VR眼动追踪推断学习者在VR讲座中的注意力分布；2）基于注意力数据在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验；3）进行为期四周的实地研究，36名大学生参与贝叶斯数据分析讲座。

Result: AttentiveLearn提高了学习者报告的学习动机和参与度，但学习成果改善的证据不明确。轶事证据表明某些参与者的注意力随时间有所改善。研究为沉浸式学习系统个性化课后支持提供了实证见解和设计启示。

Conclusion: AttentiveLearn证明了基于VR眼动追踪的个性化课后支持系统能有效提升学习动机和参与度。研究强调了课后支持在沉浸式学习中的重要性，并为未来系统设计提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Immersive learning environments such as virtual classrooms in Virtual Reality (VR) offer learners unique learning experiences, yet providing effective learner support remains a challenge. While prior HCI research has explored in-lecture support for immersive learning, little research has been conducted to provide post-lecture support, despite being critical for sustained motivation, engagement, and learning outcomes. To address this, we present AttentiveLearn, a learning ecosystem that generates personalized quizzes on a mobile learning assistant based on learners' attention distribution inferred using eye-tracking in VR lectures. We evaluated the system in a four-week field study with 36 university students attending lectures on Bayesian data analysis. AttentiveLearn improved learners' reported motivation and engagement, without conclusive evidence of learning gains. Meanwhile, anecdotal evidence suggested improvements in attention for certain participants over time. Based on our findings of the field study, we provide empirical insights and design implications for personalized post-lecture support for immersive learning systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

### [16] [Spectral dynamics reservoir computing for high-speed hardware-efficient neuromorphic processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04901)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Ryo Iguchi,Sota Hikasa,Takashi Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出频谱动力学储备计算（SDRC）框架，通过模拟滤波和包络检测利用材料响应的短时粗谱中的快速频谱动力学，在保持高速处理的同时实现强计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备计算（PRC）有潜力克服冯·诺依曼瓶颈，但实际实现面临计算性能与硬件可行性之间的张力：需要提取足够信息以获得高计算性能，同时保持硬件可行的高速架构。

Method: 提出频谱动力学储备计算（SDRC）框架，基于模拟滤波和包络检测技术，利用材料响应的短时粗谱中的快速频谱动力学，将材料的光谱流形作为高维计算资源实时使用。

Result: 在自旋波上实现SDRC，仅用56个节点就在奇偶校验和二阶非线性自回归移动平均基准任务上达到最先进水平，在语音识别实际问题上达到98.0%的高准确率。

Conclusion: SDRC框架通过有效利用材料响应的频谱动力学，在保持高速处理和最小硬件开销的同时实现强计算能力，为物理储备计算的现实世界应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) is a promising brain-inspired computing architecture for overcoming the von Neumann bottleneck by utilizing the intrinsic dynamics of physical systems. However, a major obstacle to its real-world implementation lies in the tension between extracting sufficient information for high computational performance and maintaining a hardware-feasible, high-speed architecture. Here, we report spectral dynamics reservoir computing (SDRC), a broadly applicable framework based on analogue filtering and envelope detection that bridges this gap. SDRC effectively exploits the fast spectral dynamics embedded in short-time, coarse spectra of material responses to attain strong computational capability while maintaining high-speed processing and minimal hardware overhead. This approach circumvents the need for implementation-intensive, precision-sensitive integrated circuits required in high-speed time-multiplexing measurements, while enabling real-time use of the material's spectral manifold as a high-dimensional computational resource. We implement and experimentally demonstrate SDRC applied to spin waves that achieves state-of-the-art-level performance with only 56 nodes on benchmark tasks of parity-check and second-order nonlinear autoregressive moving average, as well as high accuracy of 98.0% on a real-world problem of speech recognition.

</details>


### [17] [Rethinking Reproducibility in the Classical (HPC)-Quantum Era: Toward Workflow-Centered Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04924)
*Anna Vrtiak,Duuk Baten,Ariana Torres-Knoop*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了在经典-量子计算集成背景下科学可重复性面临的挑战，指出现有可重复性框架不足，并提出以工作流为中心的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学知识日益依赖复杂的计算过程，硬件和软件层都会影响研究结果。在计算复杂性增长的背景下，经典-量子计算集成提供了一个视角来审视科学方法如何适应，特别是关于科学验证的基本原则——可重复性。基于先前关于计算背景下持续存在的可重复性危机的警告，本文旨在研究经典（HPC）和量子计算中的挑战。

Method: 通过比较分析经典计算（HPC）和量子计算在可重复性方面的挑战。分析HPC面临的硬件依赖性、文档不足、研究文化激励不足和基础设施变化等问题；同时分析量子计算在低技术成熟度下如何放大某些挑战，并通过概率输出、硬件特定噪声和紧密的软件-硬件耦合创造新挑战。提出工作流为中心的路径，包括开发元工作流来记录过程抽象和实现上下文。

Result: 经典-量子集成揭示了一个显著模式：当前的可重复性框架证明不足，因为基础设施与结果融合在一起。量子集成作为催化剂，暴露了整个计算领域的方法论局限性。HPC尽管具有确定性，但仍面临可重复性威胁；量子计算则因概率性、噪声和硬件依赖性而面临更严峻挑战。

Conclusion: 提出以工作流为中心的解决方案，通过开发元工作流来记录过程抽象和实现上下文，为科学知识创建更强大的基础，承认复杂性而不牺牲严谨性。前进的道路涉及接受这种对科学知识理解的演变，而不是抵制它。需要逐步的文化转变，转向以工作流为中心的科学实践。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge increasingly depends on complex computational processes where both hardware and software layers can influence research outcomes. As computational complexity grows, classical-quantum integration provides a lens for examining how the scientific method adapts, particularly regarding a foundational principle of scientific validation - reproducibility. Building upon previous warnings of an ongoing reproducibility crisis in the computational context, this paper examines challenges across classical (HPC) and quantum computing. Despite its deterministic nature, HPC faces reproducibility threats from hardware dependencies, documentation inadequacies, disincentivizing research culture and infrastructure variation. Quantum computing, at low technological maturity, amplifies some challenges, while creating new ones through probabilistic outputs, hardware-specific noise, and tight software-hardware coupling. Classical-quantum integration reveals a telling pattern, where current reproducibility frameworks prove inadequate, as infrastructure blends with the results. Quantum integration serves as a catalyst exposing methodological limitations across the computational domain. We propose a workflow-centered path forward, pointing to the value of gradual cultural shift toward workflow-centered scientific practice. By developing meta-workflows that document both process abstractions and implementation contexts, we create a more robust foundation for scientific knowledge that acknowledges complexity without sacrificing rigor. The path forward involves embracing this evolution in understanding scientific knowledge rather than resisting it

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [How Effective Are Publicly Accessible Deepfake Detection Tools? A Comparative Evaluation of Open-Source and Free-to-Use Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04456)
*Michael Rettinger,Ben Beaumont,Nhien-An Le-Khac,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对六种公开可用的深度伪造检测工具进行跨范式评估，涵盖法证分析工具和AI分类器，发现法证工具召回率高但特异性差，AI分类器则相反，人类评估者显著优于所有自动化工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造图像泛滥给数字媒体真实性验证带来挑战，虽然检测算法研究丰富，但对实际使用的公开工具的实证评估仍然稀缺，需要填补这一研究空白

Method: 采用跨范式评估方法，测试六种工具：法证分析工具（InVID & WeVerify, FotoForensics, Forensically）和AI分类器（DecopyAI, FaceOnLive, Bitmind）。由有执法经验的专业调查员使用盲测协议，在包含真实、篡改和AI生成图像的数据集（DF40, CelebDF, CASIA-v2）上进行评估

Result: 三个主要发现：1) 法证工具召回率高但特异性差，AI分类器呈现相反模式；2) 人类评估者显著优于所有自动化工具；3) 人机分歧具有不对称性，在绝大多数不一致案例中人类判断占主导

Conclusion: 讨论了实践工作流程的启示，并识别了当前检测能力的关键缺口，强调需要改进工具性能并优化人机协作策略

Abstract: The proliferation of deepfake imagery poses escalating challenges for practitioners tasked with verifying digital media authenticity. While detection algorithm research is abundant, empirical evaluations of publicly accessible tools that practitioners actually use remain scarce. This paper presents the first cross-paradigm evaluation of six tools, spanning two complementary detection approaches: forensic analysis tools (InVID \& WeVerify, FotoForensics, Forensically) and AI-based classifiers (DecopyAI, FaceOnLive, Bitmind). Both tool categories were evaluated by professional investigators with law enforcement experience using blinded protocols across datasets comprising authentic, tampered, and AI-generated images sourced from DF40, CelebDF, and CASIA-v2. We report three principal findings: forensic tools exhibit high recall but poor specificity, while AI classifiers demonstrate the inverse pattern; human evaluators substantially outperform all automated tools; and human-AI disagreement is asymmetric, with human judgment prevailing in the vast majority of discordant cases. We discuss implications for practitioner workflows and identify critical gaps in current detection capabilities.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Input Guardrails: Reconstructing Cross-Agent Semantic Flows for Execution-Aware Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04469)
*Yangyang Wei,Yijie Xu,Zhenyuan Li,Xiangmin Shen,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SysName框架，将多智能体系统的防御范式从静态输入过滤转向执行感知分析，通过重构跨智能体语义流来检测复杂攻击向量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统作为复杂任务编排的标准，其自主执行和非结构化通信带来了严重安全风险（如间接提示注入），传统输入防护措施容易被绕过

Method: 提出SysName框架：提取和重构跨智能体语义流，将碎片化操作原语合成连续行为轨迹；利用监督LLM分析轨迹，检测数据流违规、控制流偏差和意图不一致等异常

Result: SysName能有效检测超过10种不同的复合攻击向量，在节点级和路径级端到端攻击检测中分别达到85.3%和66.7%的F1分数

Conclusion: SysName通过执行感知分析和跨智能体语义流重构，为多智能体系统提供了有效的安全防护框架，显著提升了复杂攻击的检测能力

Abstract: Multi-Agent System is emerging as the \textit{de facto} standard for complex task orchestration. However, its reliance on autonomous execution and unstructured inter-agent communication introduces severe risks, such as indirect prompt injection, that easily circumvent conventional input guardrails. To address this, we propose \SysName, a framework that shifts the defensive paradigm from static input filtering to execution-aware analysis. By extracting and reconstructing Cross-Agent Semantic Flows, \SysName synthesizes fragmented operational primitives into contiguous behavioral trajectories, enabling a holistic view of system activity. We leverage a Supervisor LLM to scrutinize these trajectories, identifying anomalies across data flow violations, control flow deviations, and intent inconsistencies. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that \SysName effectively detects over ten distinct compound attack vectors, achieving F1-scores of 85.3\% and 66.7\% for node-level and path-level end-to-end attack detection, respectively. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MAScope-71DC.

</details>


### [20] [Benchmark of Benchmarks: Unpacking Influence and Code Repository Quality in LLM Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04459)
*Junjie Chu,Xinyue Shen,Ye Leng,Michael Backes,Yun Shen,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对LLM安全基准测试进行多维度评估，分析学术影响力与代码质量，发现基准测试论文在学术影响力上无显著优势，且作者知名度与代码质量存在错位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM安全研究快速增长，基准测试对追踪趋势和系统比较至关重要，但缺乏对基准测试学术影响力和代码质量的系统性评估，不清楚某些基准测试为何获得突出地位

Method: 对31个基准测试和382个非基准测试进行多维度评估：学术影响力基于5个指标，代码质量基于自动化和人工评估，涵盖提示注入、越狱和幻觉三个领域

Result: 基准测试论文在学术影响力（如引用次数和密度）上无显著优势；作者知名度与论文影响力相关，但两者均与代码质量无显著相关性；代码和补充材料质量有待提升：仅39%仓库可即用，16%包含无瑕疵安装指南，仅6%涉及伦理考量

Conclusion: 基准测试的学术影响力与其代码质量存在错位，需要建立更高标准；知名研究者应带头提升基准测试的代码质量和伦理考量，以推动领域发展

Abstract: The rapid growth of research in LLM safety makes it hard to track all advances. Benchmarks are therefore crucial for capturing key trends and enabling systematic comparisons. Yet, it remains unclear why certain benchmarks gain prominence, and no systematic assessment has been conducted on their academic influence or code quality. This paper fills this gap by presenting the first multi-dimensional evaluation of the influence (based on five metrics) and code quality (based on both automated and human assessment) on LLM safety benchmarks, analyzing 31 benchmarks and 382 non-benchmarks across prompt injection, jailbreak, and hallucination. We find that benchmark papers show no significant advantage in academic influence (e.g., citation count and density) over non-benchmark papers. We uncover a key misalignment: while author prominence correlates with paper influence, neither author prominence nor paper influence shows a significant correlation with code quality. Our results also indicate substantial room for improvement in code and supplementary materials: only 39% of repositories are ready-to-use, 16% include flawless installation guides, and a mere 6% address ethical considerations. Given that the work of prominent researchers tends to attract greater attention, they need to lead the effort in setting higher standards.

</details>


### [21] [ShieldBypass: On the Persistence of Impedance Leakage Beyond EM Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04801)
*Md Sadik Awal,Md Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 电磁屏蔽无法完全防止主动射频探测攻击，攻击者可通过注入外部射频信号并分析反射响应来探测屏蔽系统内部执行状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁屏蔽广泛用于抑制辐射发射和限制被动电磁侧信道泄漏，但无法解决主动探测攻击问题。本研究旨在探究在辐射发射被屏蔽抑制的情况下，阻抗调制的反向散射是否仍然存在。

Method: 通过注入受控射频信号并分析反射响应，研究在屏蔽主要衰减频带之外的频率上是否仍能观察到状态相关的阻抗变化。使用FPGA和微控制器原型实现处理器，在三种行业标准屏蔽下评估工作负载特征。

Result: 研究发现：1) 被动电磁测量在屏蔽下失去区分能力；2) 反向散射响应在屏蔽下仍保持可分离性；3) 状态相关的阻抗变化在屏蔽主要衰减频带之外的频率上仍然可观测。

Conclusion: 主动射频探测能够暴露屏蔽系统的执行相关行为，表明需要在硬件安全评估流程中考虑基于主动阻抗的探测攻击。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) shielding is widely used to suppress radiated emissions and limit passive EM side-channel leakage. However, shielding does not address active probing, where an adversary injects external radio-frequency (RF) signals and observes the device's reflective response. This work studies whether such impedance-modulated backscattering persists when radiated emissions are suppressed by shielding. By injecting controlled RF signals and analyzing the reflections, we demonstrate that state-dependent impedance variations remain observable at frequencies outside the shields' primary attenuation band. Using processors implemented on FPGA and microcontroller prototypes, and evaluating workload profiles under three industry-standard shields, we find that passive EM measurements lose discriminative power under shielding, while backscattering responses remain separable. These results indicate that active RF probing can expose execution-dependent behavior even in shielded systems, motivating the need to consider active impedance-based probing within hardware security evaluation flows.

</details>


### [22] [When Denoising Becomes Unsigning: Theoretical and Empirical Analysis of Watermark Fragility Under Diffusion-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04696)
*Fai Gu,Qiyu Tang,Te Wen,Emily Davis,Finn Carter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 扩散编辑会破坏传统鲁棒水印：扩散模型通过注入高斯噪声和去噪过程，将水印视为高频噪声而衰减，导致解码错误率接近随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩散编辑与鲁棒水印之间的微妙但日益重要的相互作用。扩散编辑可能无意中破坏甚至绕过那些为抵抗传统失真而设计的鲁棒水印机制。

Method: 建立扩散编辑的统一视图：在潜在空间注入高斯噪声，然后通过学习的去噪动力学投影回自然图像流形。使用信息论工具形式化水印退化过程，证明水印载荷与编辑输出之间的互信息随编辑强度增加而衰减至零。设计实验协议，测试代表性水印方法和扩散编辑器。

Result: 理论证明：对于广泛的像素级水印编码器/解码器，水印载荷与编辑输出之间的互信息随编辑强度增加而衰减至零，导致解码错误接近随机猜测。实验验证了扩散编辑对多种水印方法的破坏效果。

Conclusion: 扩散编辑会系统性破坏传统鲁棒水印。需要重新设计水印方案以在生成式变换时代保持有效性，并讨论了伦理影响、负责任披露规范和具体设计指南。

Abstract: Robust invisible watermarking systems aim to embed imperceptible payloads that remain decodable after common post-processing such as JPEG compression, cropping, and additive noise. In parallel, diffusion-based image editing has rapidly matured into a default transformation layer for modern content pipelines, enabling instruction-based editing, object insertion and composition, and interactive geometric manipulation. This paper studies a subtle but increasingly consequential interaction between these trends: diffusion-based editing procedures may unintentionally compromise, and in extreme cases practically bypass, robust watermarking mechanisms that were explicitly engineered to survive conventional distortions. We develop a unified view of diffusion editors that (i) inject substantial Gaussian noise in a latent space and (ii) project back to the natural image manifold via learned denoising dynamics. Under this view, watermark payloads behave as low-energy, high-frequency signals that are systematically attenuated by the forward diffusion step and then treated as nuisance variation by the reverse generative process. We formalize this degradation using information-theoretic tools, proving that for broad classes of pixel-level watermark encoders/decoders the mutual information between the watermark payload and the edited output decays toward zero as the editing strength increases, yielding decoding error close to random guessing. We complement the theory with a realistic hypothetical experimental protocol and tables spanning representative watermarking methods and representative diffusion editors. Finally, we discuss ethical implications, responsible disclosure norms, and concrete design guidelines for watermarking schemes that remain meaningful in the era of generative transformations.

</details>


### [23] [Efficient Privacy-Preserving Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication Using Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04742)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Wujie Wen,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个将同态加密与稀疏矩阵向量乘法高效集成的隐私保护计算框架，提出CSSC压缩格式优化加密稀疏矩阵计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵向量乘法是科学计算、数据分析和机器学习的基础操作，处理敏感数据时隐私保护至关重要。同态加密虽能实现隐私保护计算，但在稀疏矩阵向量乘法中的应用尚未得到充分解决，需要同时应对计算效率和数据隐私的双重挑战。

Method: 提出名为压缩稀疏排序列的CSSC新型压缩矩阵格式，专门为优化加密稀疏矩阵计算而设计。该格式通过保持稀疏性和实现高效密文打包，显著减少存储和计算开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法在处理时间和内存使用方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了隐私保护稀疏矩阵向量乘法的发展，为联邦学习、加密数据库、科学计算等安全应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) is a fundamental operation in scientific computing, data analysis, and machine learning. When the data being processed are sensitive, preserving privacy becomes critical, and homomorphic encryption (HE) has emerged as a leading approach for addressing this challenge. Although HE enables privacy-preserving computation, its application to SpMV has remained largely unaddressed. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first framework that efficiently integrates HE with SpMV, addressing the dual challenges of computational efficiency and data privacy. In particular, we introduce a novel compressed matrix format, named Compressed Sparse Sorted Column (CSSC), which is specifically designed to optimize encrypted sparse matrix computations. By preserving sparsity and enabling efficient ciphertext packing, CSSC significantly reduces storage and computational overhead. Our experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves significant gains in both processing time and memory usage. This study advances privacy-preserving SpMV and lays the groundwork for secure applications in federated learning, encrypted databases, scientific computing, and beyond.

</details>


### [24] [Osmosis Distillation: Model Hijacking with the Fewest Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04859)
*Yuchen Shi,Huajie Chen,Heng Xu,Zhiquan Liu,Jialiang Shen,Chi Liu,Shuai Zhou,Tianqing Zhu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Osmosis Distillation（OD）攻击的新型模型劫持策略，揭示了在迁移学习中使用数据集蒸馏生成的合成数据集时存在的安全威胁，攻击者只需在合成数据集中添加少量中毒样本即可实施攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习和数据集蒸馏的结合在评估中表现出良好性能，但研究人员发现其中存在未被发现的安全威胁：在使用数据集蒸馏方法生成的合成数据集进行迁移学习时，攻击者可以通过在合成数据集中添加少量中毒样本实施模型劫持攻击。

Method: 提出了Osmosis Distillation（OD）攻击，这是一种针对深度学习模型的新型模型劫持策略，使用最少的样本实现攻击。该方法通过蒸馏渗透集（distilled osmosis set）实现跨不同模型架构的模型劫持。

Result: 在各种数据集上的综合评估表明，OD攻击在隐藏任务中实现了高攻击成功率，同时在原始任务中保持了高模型效用。蒸馏渗透集使得模型劫持能够跨越不同的模型架构，在迁移学习中实现显著的攻击性能和模型效用。

Conclusion: 必须提高对在迁移学习中使用第三方合成数据集的安全意识，因为其中存在模型劫持攻击的严重安全威胁。OD攻击揭示了这种威胁的严重性，需要引起研究社区和从业者的重视。

Abstract: Transfer learning is devised to leverage knowledge from pre-trained models to solve new tasks with limited data and computational resources. Meanwhile, dataset distillation has emerged to synthesize a compact dataset that preserves critical information from the original large dataset. Therefore, a combination of transfer learning and dataset distillation offers promising performance in evaluations. However, a non-negligible security threat remains undiscovered in transfer learning using synthetic datasets generated by dataset distillation methods, where an adversary can perform a model hijacking attack with only a few poisoned samples in the synthetic dataset. To reveal this threat, we propose Osmosis Distillation (OD) attack, a novel model hijacking strategy that targets deep learning models using the fewest samples. Comprehensive evaluations on various datasets demonstrate that the OD attack attains high attack success rates in hidden tasks while preserving high model utility in original tasks. Furthermore, the distilled osmosis set enables model hijacking across diverse model architectures, allowing model hijacking in transfer learning with considerable attack performance and model utility. We argue that awareness of using third-party synthetic datasets in transfer learning must be raised.

</details>


### [25] [AgentSCOPE: Evaluating Contextual Privacy Across Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04902)
*Ivoline C. Ngong,Keerthiram Murugesan,Swanand Kadhe,Justin D. Weisz,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Privacy Flow Graph框架，基于情境完整性理论分解智能体执行流程，发现现有隐私评估只关注输入输出边界，而智能体管道中的中间信息流存在大量隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统越来越多地代表用户执行任务，访问日历、邮件和个人文件等敏感信息。现有的隐私评估主要关注输入和输出边界，但忽略了任务执行过程中的中间信息流（如智能体查询和工具响应），这些环节同样存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出Privacy Flow Graph框架，基于情境完整性理论将智能体执行分解为一系列信息流，每个流都用五个CI参数标注，能够追踪隐私违规到其源头。同时构建了AgentSCOPE基准测试，包含8个监管领域的62个多工具场景，每个管道阶段都有真实标签。

Result: 对7个最先进的LLM进行评估显示，超过80%的场景在管道中存在隐私违规，即使最终输出看起来干净（24%）。大多数违规发生在工具响应阶段，API会不加区分地返回敏感数据。这表明仅进行输出级评估会严重低估智能体系统的隐私风险。

Conclusion: 智能体管道的每个边界都是潜在的隐私违规点，需要独立评估。Privacy Flow Graph框架能够有效识别和追踪中间信息流中的隐私泄露，现有仅关注输入输出的评估方法不足以反映智能体系统的真实隐私风险。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly acting on users' behalf, accessing calendars, email, and personal files to complete everyday tasks. Privacy evaluation for these systems has focused on the input and output boundaries, but each task involves several intermediate information flows, from agent queries to tool responses, that are not currently evaluated. We argue that every boundary in an agentic pipeline is a site of potential privacy violation and must be assessed independently. To support this, we introduce the Privacy Flow Graph, a Contextual Integrity-grounded framework that decomposes agentic execution into a sequence of information flows, each annotated with the five CI parameters, and traces violations to their point of origin. We present AgentSCOPE, a benchmark of 62 multi-tool scenarios across eight regulatory domains with ground truth at every pipeline stage. Our evaluation across seven state-of-the-art LLMs show that privacy violations in the pipeline occur in over 80% of scenarios, even when final outputs appear clean (24%), with most violations arising at the tool-response stage where APIs return sensitive data indiscriminately. These results indicate that output-level evaluation alone substantially underestimates the privacy risk of agentic systems.

</details>


### [26] [Modification to Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04952)
*Sona Alex,Bian Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FHMRS存在安全漏洞，mFHMRS通过修改方案来缓解该漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现FHMRS方案存在安全漏洞，需要提出改进方案来增强安全性

Method: 分析FHMRS的安全漏洞，并提出修改后的mFHMRS方案来缓解该漏洞

Result: 识别了FHMRS的安全问题，设计了mFHMRS作为改进方案

Conclusion: mFHMRS通过修改原始方案有效缓解了FHMRS的安全漏洞

Abstract: This document details the Fully Homomorphic Modified Rivest Scheme (FHMRS), a security issue in FHMRS, and a modification to FHMRS (mFHMRS) to mitigate the security issue.

</details>


### [27] [A Practical Post-Quantum Distributed Ledger Protocol for Financial Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05005)
*Yeoh Wei Zhu,Naresh Goud Boddu,Yao Ma,Shaltiel Eloul,Giulio Golinelli,Yash Satsangi,Rob Otter,Kaushik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于格的后量子加密账本交易方案，解决金融机构隐私与审计需求，引入新的承诺等式证明和紧凑范围证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融机构存在效率问题，分布式账本技术可解决但面临隐私挑战。现有Ring-CT模型隐私协议不适合金融机构采用，需要满足保密性和可审计性要求的方案。

Method: 提出基于格的后量子交易方案，利用多种零知识证明技术，引入新的承诺等式证明方法（无需打开原承诺），构建紧凑范围证明，支持单/多资产公开可验证交易。

Result: 构建了适用于金融机构的公开可验证交易方案，支持单资产和多资产场景，提供了安全性分析，所提出的证明技术具有独立研究价值。

Conclusion: 提出的基于格的后量子加密账本方案解决了金融机构对隐私和审计的双重需求，引入的新证明方法为相关领域提供了有价值的技术贡献。

Abstract: Traditional financial institutions face inefficiencies that can be addressed by distributed ledger technology. However, a primary barrier to adoption is the privacy concerns surrounding publicly available transaction data. Existing private protocols for distributed ledger that focus on the Ring-CT model are not suitable for adoption for financial institutions. We propose a post-quantum, lattice-based transaction scheme for encrypted ledgers which better aligns with institutions' requirements for confidentiality and audit-ability. The construction leverages various zero-knowledge proof techniques, and introduces a new method for equating two commitment messages, without the capability to open one of the commitment during the re-commitment. Subsequently, we build a publicly verifiable transaction scheme that is efficient for single or multi-assets, by introducing a new compact range-proof. We then provide a security analysis of it. The techniques used and the proofs constructed could be of independent interest.

</details>


### [28] [Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation (GELO)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05035)
*Anatoly Belikov,Ilya Fedotov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GELO是一种轻量级的隐私保护推理协议，通过每批次可逆混合隐藏隐藏状态，防止共享加速器上的KV缓存和隐藏状态被窃取，在保持输出精度的同时仅增加20-30%延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在共享加速器上部署时，攻击者可通过读取设备内存观察KV缓存和隐藏状态，威胁开源模型的提示隐私。现有密码学方法（如MPC和FHE）速度太慢，静态混淆方案在多轮统计攻击下会失效。

Method: GELO采用每批次新鲜可逆混合：TEE为每个卸载的投影采样随机矩阵A，计算U=AH，将U和权重W卸载到加速器，返回后应用A⁻¹恢复正确输出。引入两种防御：(i)非正交混合掩蔽Gram矩阵，(ii)正交混合加高能量"屏蔽"向量污染高阶统计。

Result: 在Llama-2 7B上，GELO能精确保持float32输出，接近低精度基线性能，卸载主要矩阵乘法仅增加20-30%延迟开销，并能抵御一系列ICA/BSS和基于锚点的攻击。

Conclusion: GELO提供了一种实用的隐私保护推理方案，通过轻量级可逆混合在保持模型性能的同时有效防御共享加速器上的观察攻击，平衡了隐私保护与推理效率的需求。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly served on shared accelerators where an adversary with read access to device memory can observe KV caches and hidden states, threatening prompt privacy for open-source models. Cryptographic protections such as MPC and FHE offer strong guarantees but remain one to two orders of magnitude too slow for interactive inference, while static obfuscation schemes break under multi-run statistical attacks once the model is known. We present GELO (Good-Enough LLM Obfuscation), a lightweight protocol for privacy-preserving inference that limits information leakage from untrusted accelerator observations by hiding hidden states with fresh, per-batch invertible mixing. For each offloaded projection, the TEE samples a random matrix A, forms $U = AH$, offloads U and weights W to the accelerator, and then applies $A^-1$ on return, so that $A^-1 ((AH)W ) = HW$ and outputs are unchanged. Because mixing is never reused across batches, the attacker faces only a single-batch blind source separation problem. We analyze information leakage and introduce two practical defenses: (i) non-orthogonal mixing to mask Gram matrices, and (ii) orthogonal mixing augmented with a small fraction of high-energy "shield" vectors that pollute higher-order statistics. On Llama-2 7B, GELO preserves float32 outputs exactly, closely matches low-precision baselines, offloads the dominant matrix multiplications with about 20-30% latency overhead, and defeats a range of ICA/BSS and anchor-based attacks.

</details>


### [29] [Robust Single-message Shuffle Differential Privacy Protocol for Accurate Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05073)
*Xiaoguang Li,Hanyi Wang,Yaowei Huang,Jungang Yang,Qingqing Ye,Haonan Yan,Ke Pan,Zhe Sun,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASP协议用于洗牌差分隐私下的数值数据分布估计，在效用、消息复杂度和抗数据投毒攻击鲁棒性三方面优于基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洗牌差分隐私协议主要关注分类数据的频率估计，但数值数据更为普遍且许多应用需要估计具有序数性质的数据分布。现有方法在效用、消息复杂度和抗攻击鲁棒性三方面无法同时取得优异表现。

Method: 提出ASP协议：1) 通过更紧的互信息界进行参数优化设计随机化器；2) 设计EMAS算法准确恢复分布并增强鲁棒性；3) 提出新的评估框架量化不同攻击目标下的鲁棒性。

Result: ASP在三个指标上均优于基线协议：在小ε值时，效用提升一个数量级，消息复杂度最小，鲁棒性比基线方法高三倍以上。

Conclusion: ASP协议在纯洗牌模型下实现了数值数据分布估计的高效用、低消息复杂度和强鲁棒性，为洗牌差分隐私在数值数据上的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Shuffler-based differential privacy (shuffle-DP) is a privacy paradigm providing high utility by involving a shuffler to permute noisy report from users. Existing shuffle-DP protocols mainly focus on the design of shuffler-based categorical frequency oracle (SCFO) for frequency estimation on categorical data. However, numerical data is a more prevalent type and many real-world applications depend on the estimation of data distribution with ordinal nature. In this paper, we study the distribution estimation under pure shuffle model, which is a prevalent shuffle-DP framework without strong security assumptions. We initially attempt to transplant existing SCFOs and the naïve distribution recovery technique to this task, and demonstrate that these baseline protocols cannot simultaneously achieve outstanding performance in three metrics: 1) utility, 2) message complexity; and 3) robustness to data poisoning attacks. Therefore, we further propose a novel single-message \textit{adaptive shuffler-based piecewise} (ASP) protocol with high utility and robustness. In ASP, we first develop a randomizer by parameter optimization using our proposed tighter bound of mutual information. We also design an \textit{Expectation Maximization with Adaptive Smoothing} (EMAS) algorithm to accurately recover distribution with enhanced robustness. To quantify robustness, we propose a new evaluation framework to examine robustness under different attack targets, enabling us to comprehensively understand the protocol resilience under various adversarial scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASP outperforms baseline protocols in all three metrics. Especially under small $ε$ values, ASP achieves an order of magnitude improvement in utility with minimal message complexity, and exhibits over threefold robustness compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [30] [Lambda-randomization: multi-dimensional randomized response made easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05261)
*Nicolas Ruiz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Lambda-randomization协议，通过参数化随机化矩阵解决多维随机响应中的维度灾难问题，降低计算成本并保持分布估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机响应作为本地匿名化方法在多维数据处理中存在维度灾难问题：随着属性数量增加，计算成本急剧上升，分布估计准确性下降。需要解决这一传统限制。

Method: 提出Lambda-randomization协议：1) 引入简单直观的随机化矩阵参数化方法；2) 仅使用三个元素：每个属性在0到1之间的参数、单位矩阵和全1向量；3) 基于新的理论洞察开发低计算成本的多变量分布估计方法。

Result: Lambda-randomization协议能够避免传统多维随机响应的维度灾难问题，显著降低计算成本，同时保持从匿名化数据中检索真实分布的无偏估计能力。

Conclusion: 通过参数化随机化矩阵的新方法，成功解决了多维随机响应中的计算效率和准确性挑战，为高维数据的隐私保护分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Randomized response is a popular local anonymization approach that can deliver anonymized multi-dimensional data sets with rigorous privacy guarantees. At the same time, it can ensure validity for exploratory analysis and machine learning tasks as, under fairly general conditions, unbiased estimates of the underlying true distributions can be retrieved. However, and like for many other anonymization techniques, one of the main pitfalls of this approach is the curse of dimensionality. When coping with data sets with many attributes, one quickly runs into unsustainable computational costs for estimating true distributions, as well as a degradation in their accuracies. Relying on new theoretical insights developed in this paper, we propose an approach to multi-dimensional randomized response that avoids these traditional limitations. From simple yet intuitive parameterizations of the randomization matrices that we introduce, we develop a protocol called Lambda-randomization that entails low computational costs to retrieve estimates of multivariate distributions, and that makes use of solely three simple elements: a set of parameters ranging between 0 and 1 (one per attribute of the data set), the identity matrix, and the all-ones vector. We also present an empirical application to illustrate the proposed protocol.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [31] [GAIDE: Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04463)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAIDE是一种基于图注意力掩码的神经引导采样器，通过编码运动规划问题的空间结构和机器人本体信息来提高采样效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的运动规划算法在高维构型空间中采样效率低下，现有神经引导采样器难以有效编码运动规划问题固有的空间结构

Method: 提出GAIDE方法，将规划问题的空间结构和机器人本体信息表示为图，并通过注意力掩码机制集成到基于Transformer的神经采样器中

Result: 与均匀采样、手工设计的启发式采样和现有神经引导采样方法相比，GAIDE显著提高了规划效率和成功率

Conclusion: GAIDE通过有效编码空间结构和机器人本体信息，为高维运动规划问题提供了更高效的神经引导采样解决方案

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning algorithms are widely used for motion planning of robotic manipulators, but they often struggle with sample inefficiency in high-dimensional configuration spaces due to their reliance on uniform or hand-crafted informed sampling primitives. Neural informed samplers address this limitation by learning the sampling distribution from prior planning experience to guide the motion planner towards planning goal. However, existing approaches often struggle to encode the spatial structure inherent in motion planning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning (GAIDE), a neural informed sampler that leverages both the spatial structure of the planning problem and the robotic manipulator's embodiment to guide the planning algorithm. GAIDE represents these structures as a graph and integrates it into a transformer-based neural sampler through attention masking. We evaluate GAIDE against baseline state-of-the-art sampling-based planners using uniform sampling, hand-crafted informed sampling, and neural informed sampling primitives. Evaluation results demonstrate that GAIDE improves planning efficiency and success rate.

</details>


### [32] [Adaptive Policy Switching of Two-Wheeled Differential Robots for Traversing over Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04761)
*Haruki Izawa,Takeshi Takai,Shingo Kitano,Mikita Miyaguchi,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过机器人姿态数据（3D方向）进行地形分类，为自适应策略切换提供基础，在模拟熔岩管环境中实现了超过98%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球熔岩管探测需要机器人自主导航，预训练策略无法覆盖所有地形条件，因此需要基于当前地形特征自适应切换专门化策略。

Method: 使用PPO微调预训练策略，在模拟熔岩管环境中收集机器人在平坦和崎岖地形上的3D方向数据，分析俯仰角标准差的地形差异，采用高斯混合模型在不同窗口大小下进行地形分类评估。

Result: 机器人俯仰角标准差在两种地形间有明显差异；使用70步窗口时，高斯混合模型的地形分类准确率超过98%。

Conclusion: 短期方向数据足以进行可靠的地形估计，为自适应策略切换提供了基础，支持月球熔岩管探测中的自主导航。

Abstract: Exploring lunar lava tubes requires robots to traverse without human intervention. Because pre-trained policies cannot fully cover all possible terrain conditions, our goal is to enable adaptive policy switching, where the robot selects an appropriate terrain-specialized model based on its current terrain features. This study investigates whether terrain types can be estimated effectively using posture-related observations collected during navigation. We fine-tuned a pre-trained policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and then collected the robot's 3D orientation data as it moved across flat and rough terrain in a simulated lava-tube environment. Our analysis revealed that the standard deviation of the robot's pitch data shows a clear difference between these two terrain types. Using Gaussian mixture models (GMM), we evaluated terrain classification across various window sizes. An accuracy of more than 98% was achieved when using a 70-step window. The result suggests that short-term orientation data are sufficient for reliable terrain estimation, providing a foundation for adaptive policy switching.

</details>


### [33] [LLM-Guided Decentralized Exploration with Self-Organizing Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04762)
*Hiroaki Kawashima,Shun Ikejima,Takeshi Takai,Mikita Miyaguchi,Yasuharu Kunii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合自组织团队形成和基于LLM的目标决策的多机器人探索方法，通过模拟验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个机器人感知能力有限且容错性不足时，需要多个机器人组队以增强观测范围和可靠性。传统集中式控制缺乏鲁棒性和灵活性，需要自主的团队形成和目标决策机制。

Method: 提出两种算法结合的方法：(1) 自组织算法实现自主动态的多团队形成；(2) 基于大语言模型(LLM)的算法让每个团队自主确定下一个探索目标。与经典前沿方法和深度强化学习方法形成对比。

Result: 通过包含数十到数百个机器人的模拟实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合自组织团队形成和基于LLM的目标决策方法能够实现高效的多机器人自主探索，相比传统方法更具鲁棒性和灵活性。

Abstract: When individual robots have limited sensing capabilities or insufficient fault tolerance, it becomes necessary for multiple robots to form teams during exploration, thereby increasing the collective observation range and reliability. Traditionally, swarm formation has often been managed by a central controller; however, from the perspectives of robustness and flexibility, it is preferable for the swarm to operate autonomously even in the absence of centralized control. In addition, the determination of exploration targets for each team is crucial for efficient exploration in such multi-team exploration scenarios. This study therefore proposes an exploration method that combines (1) an algorithm for self-organization, enabling the autonomous and dynamic formation of multiple teams, and (2) an algorithm that allows each team to autonomously determine its next exploration target (destination). In particular, for (2), this study explores a novel strategy based on large language models (LLMs), while classical frontier-based methods and deep reinforcement learning approaches have been widely studied. The effectiveness of the proposed method was validated through simulations involving tens to hundreds of robots.

</details>


### [34] [U-OBCA: Uncertainty-Aware Optimization-Based Collision Avoidance via Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Chance Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04914)
*Zehao Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Han Zhang,Jingchuan Wang,Weidong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: U-OBCA：一种不确定性感知的优化避障框架，通过机会约束处理多边形机器人与障碍物的碰撞风险，避免几何简化，减少保守性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性感知规划器通常使用圆形或椭圆等简单几何基元近似多边形机器人和障碍物，这显著缩小了可行空间，导致过度保守的轨迹，在狭窄环境中甚至导致规划失败。此外，许多方法依赖特定的噪声分布假设，这些假设在实践中可能不成立，限制了性能保证。

Method: 扩展优化避障（OBCA）框架为不确定性感知的U-OBCA方法。该方法通过制定OBCA机会约束，显式考虑多边形机器人与障碍物之间的碰撞风险，避免几何简化。在温和分布假设下，将这些概率约束收紧为确定性非线性约束，可通过标准数值优化求解器高效求解。

Result: 通过理论分析、数值仿真和真实世界实验验证了所提方法。结果表明，U-OBCA显著减轻了轨迹规划中的保守性，与现有基线方法相比实现了更高的导航效率，特别是在狭窄和杂乱的环境中。

Conclusion: U-OBCA框架成功解决了现有不确定性感知规划器的局限性，通过避免几何简化并采用温和分布假设，在保证安全的同时减少了不必要的保守性，提高了狭窄复杂环境中的导航效率。

Abstract: Uncertainties arising from localization error, trajectory prediction errors of the moving obstacles and environmental disturbances pose significant challenges to robot's safe navigation. Existing uncertainty-aware planners often approximate polygon-shaped robots and obstacles using simple geometric primitives such as circles or ellipses. Though computationally convenient, these approximations substantially shrink the feasible space, leading to overly conservative trajectories and even planning failure in narrow environments. In addition, many such methods rely on specific assumptions about noise distributions, which may not hold in practice and thus limit their performance guarantees. To address these limitations, we extend the Optimization-Based Collision Avoidance (OBCA) framework to an uncertainty-aware formulation, termed \emph{U-OBCA}. The proposed method explicitly accounts for the collision risk between polygon-shaped robots and obstacles by formulating OBCA-based chance constraints, and hence avoiding geometric simplifications and reducing unnecessary conservatism. These probabilistic constraints are further tightened into deterministic nonlinear constraints under mild distributional assumptions, which can be solved efficiently by standard numerical optimization solvers. The proposed approach is validated through theoretical analysis, numerical simulations and real-world experiments. The results demonstrate that U-OBCA significantly mitigates the conservatism in trajectory planning and achieves higher navigation efficiency compared to existing baseline methods, particularly in narrow and cluttered environments.

</details>


### [35] [PTLD: Sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04531)
*Rosy Chen,Mustafa Mukadam,Michael Kaess,Tingfan Wu,Francois R Hogan,Jitendra Malik,Akash Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PTLD是一种无需触觉模拟的sim-to-real方法，通过特权传感器收集真实世界触觉数据，蒸馏出触觉状态估计器，显著提升模拟训练的机械手操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉灵巧操作对家庭自动化任务至关重要，但学习有效控制策略面临挑战。模仿学习需要高质量示教数据难以获取，而强化学习在模拟中训练又面临触觉观测快速真实模拟的困难

Method: 提出PTLD：sim-to-real特权触觉潜在蒸馏方法。不模拟触觉传感器或依赖纯本体感知策略，而是利用真实世界中的特权传感器收集触觉策略数据，然后蒸馏出基于触觉输入的鲁棒状态估计器

Result: PTLD显著改善了模拟训练的本体感知操作策略。在基准手内旋转任务中，相比纯本体感知策略提升了182%。在触觉手内重定向任务中，达到目标的数量比纯本体感知提高了57%

Conclusion: PTLD提供了一种无需触觉模拟的有效方法，通过特权传感器数据蒸馏触觉状态估计器，成功将模拟训练的策略与真实世界触觉感知结合，显著提升了灵巧操作性能

Abstract: Tactile dexterous manipulation is essential to automating complex household tasks, yet learning effective control policies remains a challenge. While recent work has relied on imitation learning, obtaining high quality demonstrations for multi-fingered hands via robot teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching is prohibitive. Alternatively, with reinforcement we can learn skills in simulation, but fast and realistic simulation of tactile observations is challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce PTLD: sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation, a novel approach to learning tactile manipulation skills without requiring tactile simulation. Instead of simulating tactile sensors or relying purely on proprioceptive policies to transfer zero-shot sim-to-real, our key idea is to leverage privileged sensors in the real world to collect real-world tactile policy data. This data is then used to distill a robust state estimator that operates on tactile input. We demonstrate from our experiments that PTLD can be used to improve proprioceptive manipulation policies trained in simulation significantly by incorporating tactile sensing. On the benchmark in-hand rotation task, PTLD achieves a 182% improvement over a proprioception only policy. We also show that PTLD enables learning the challenging task of tactile in-hand reorientation where we see a 57% improvement in the number of goals reached over using proprioception alone. Website: https://akashsharma02.github.io/ptld-website/.

</details>


### [36] [Many-RRT*: Robust Joint-Space Trajectory Planning for Serial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04547)
*Theodore M. Belmont,Benjamin A. Christie,Anton Netchaev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Many-RRT*：一种并行规划到多个目标的RRT*-Connect扩展方法，通过同时从多个逆运动学解生长独立树，解决高自由度机械臂关节空间规划中的多臂赌博机问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度串联机械臂在关节空间进行运动规划面临挑战，因为单一任务空间末端执行器姿态对应多个配置空间状态（逆运动学多解），形成多臂赌博机问题。传统方法选择错误的关节空间目标会导致次优轨迹甚至规划失败。

Method: 提出Many-RRT*方法，扩展RRT*-Connect算法，并行规划到多个目标。通过生成多个逆运动学解，从这些目标配置同时生长独立树，与单个起始树并行扩展。这种方法确保计算资源不会浪费在次优的逆运动学解上。

Result: 在不同机器人形态和多样化障碍环境的实验评估中，Many-RRT*在相同运行时间内提供更高质量的轨迹（成本降低44.5%），成功率显著更高（100% vs 次优方法的1.6%），且不牺牲运行时间性能。

Conclusion: Many-RRT*通过并行规划到多个逆运动学解，有效解决了高自由度机械臂关节空间规划中的多目标选择问题，保持了鲁棒收敛性和渐近最优性，显著提升了规划质量和成功率。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high degree-of-freedom (DoF) serial manipulators necessitates the use of swift, sampling-based motion planners for high-dimensional spaces. While sampling-based planners like the Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) are widely used, planning in the manipulator's joint space presents significant challenges due to non-invertible forward kinematics. A single task-space end-effector pose can correspond to multiple configuration-space states, creating a multi-arm bandit problem for the planner. In complex environments, simply choosing the wrong joint space goal can result in suboptimal trajectories or even failure to find a viable plan. To address this planning problem, we propose Many-RRT*: an extension of RRT*-Connect that plans to multiple goals in parallel. By generating multiple IK solutions and growing independent trees from these goal configurations simultaneously alongside a single start tree, Many-RRT* ensures that computational effort is not wasted on suboptimal IK solutions. This approach maintains robust convergence and asymptotic optimality. Experimental evaluations across robot morphologies and diverse obstacle environments demonstrate that Many-RRT* provides higher quality trajectories (44.5% lower cost in the same runtime) with a significantly higher success rate (100% vs. the next best of 1.6%) than previous RRT iterations without compromising on runtime performance.

</details>


### [37] [From Code to Road: A Vehicle-in-the-Loop and Digital Twin-Based Framework for Central Car Server Testing in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05279)
*Chengdong Wu,Sven Kirchner,Nils Purschke,Axel Torschmied,Norbert Kroth,Yinglei Song,André Schamschurko,Erik Leo Haß,Kuo-Yi Chao,Yi Zhang,Nenad Petrovic,Alois C. Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于车辆在环和数字孪生技术的集中式E/E架构汽车软件测试框架，将物理测试车辆与虚拟仿真环境耦合，为自动驾驶算法验证提供安全、可重复、真实且经济高效的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯虚拟仿真难以准确捕捉所有现实因素，传统车辆在环方法存在局限性。需要为集中式E/E架构的自动驾驶软件提供更有效的测试方法，减少硬件集成需求，降低开发成本。

Method: 结合车辆在环和数字孪生技术，将物理测试车辆（在测功机试验台上）与其同步的虚拟对应物耦合在仿真环境中。自动驾驶软件直接在车辆硬件上运行，无需刷写和中间层，实现无缝虚拟-物理集成。

Result: 实验案例研究表明该框架在不同测试场景中有效。与传统方法相比，消除了单独测试物理ECU及其通信协议的需求，减少了早期开发阶段的全硬件集成需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为集中式E/E架构的自动驾驶软件测试提供了创新解决方案，具有减少开发和集成工作量的潜力，能够准确反映集中式E/E行为，支持混合测试环境。

Abstract: Simulation is one of the most essential parts in the development stage of automotive software. However, purely virtual simulations often struggle to accurately capture all real-world factors due to limitations in modeling. To address this challenge, this work presents a test framework for automotive software on the centralized E/E architecture, which is a central car server in our case, based on Vehicle-in-the-Loop (ViL) and digital twin technology. The framework couples a physical test vehicle on a dynamometer test bench with its synchronized virtual counterpart in a simulation environment. Our approach provides a safe, reproducible, realistic, and cost-effective platform for validating autonomous driving algorithms with a centralized architecture. This test method eliminates the need to test individual physical ECUs and their communication protocols separately. In contrast to traditional ViL methods, the proposed framework runs the full autonomous driving software directly on the vehicle hardware after the simulation process, eliminating flashing and intermediate layers while enabling seamless virtual-physical integration and accurately reflecting centralized E/E behavior. In addition, incorporating mixed testing in both simulated and physical environments reduces the need for full hardware integration during the early stages of automotive development. Experimental case studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework in different test scenarios. These findings highlight the potential to reduce development and integration efforts for testing autonomous driving pipelines in the future.

</details>


### [38] [From Local Corrections to Generalized Skills: Improving Neuro-Symbolic Policies with MEMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04560)
*Benjamin A. Christie,Yinlong Dai,Mohammad Bararjanianbahnamiri,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEMO：通过收集、聚类和重述多人多任务的自然语言修正反馈，构建检索增强的技能库，使机器人能够动态扩展技能并泛化到新任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号框架依赖预定义技能，当机器人无法生成适合当前任务的技能时策略会失败。人类能够直观解释失败原因，但简单的文本记忆方法无法实现泛化

Method: 提出MEMO框架：收集多人多任务的自然语言修正反馈，通过聚类和重述合成更通用的文本指导和编码技能模板，构建检索增强的技能库。运行时检索相关文本和代码，使策略能够生成新技能并基于多任务人类反馈进行推理

Result: 实验表明，MEMO通过将局部反馈聚合为通用技能模板，能够泛化到现有基线方法无法处理的新任务

Conclusion: 利用人类反馈构建动态技能库的方法能够有效解决机器人技能约束问题，通过自然语言修正的聚合和泛化实现对新任务的适应

Abstract: Recent works use a neuro-symbolic framework for general manipulation policies. The advantage of this framework is that -- by applying off-the-shelf vision and language models -- the robot can break complex tasks down into semantic subtasks. However, the fundamental bottleneck is that the robot needs skills to ground these subtasks into embodied motions. Skills can take many forms (e.g., trajectory snippets, motion primitives, coded functions), but regardless of their form skills act as a constraint. The high-level policy can only ground its language reasoning through the available skills; if the robot cannot generate the right skill for the current task, its policy will fail. We propose to address this limitation -- and dynamically expand the robot's skills -- by leveraging user feedback. When a robot fails, humans can intuitively explain what went wrong (e.g., ``no, go higher''). While a simple approach is to recall this exact text the next time the robot faces a similar situation, we hypothesize that by collecting, clustering, and re-phrasing natural language corrections across multiple users and tasks, we can synthesize more general text guidance and coded skill templates. Applying this hypothesis we develop Memory Enhanced Manipulation (MEMO). MEMO builds and maintains a retrieval-augmented skillbook gathered from human feedback and task successes. At run time, MEMO retrieves relevant text and code from this skillbook, enabling the robot's policy to generate new skills while reasoning over multi-task human feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that using MEMO to aggregate local feedback into general skill templates enables generalization to novel tasks where existing baselines fall short. See supplemental material here: https://collab.me.vt.edu/memo

</details>


### [39] [Accelerating Sampling-Based Control via Learned Linear Koopman Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05385)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度Koopman算子的高效模型预测路径积分控制框架，通过学习的线性DKO模型替代非线性动力学进行轨迹传播，在保持控制性能的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MPPI控制框架在处理复杂非线性动力学系统时计算效率较低，需要更高效的轨迹传播方法以实现实时控制。

Method: 使用学习的线性深度Koopman算子模型替代非线性动力学进行轨迹传播，直接从交互数据学习DKO动力学，无需解析系统模型，构建MPPI-DK控制器。

Result: 在摆锤平衡和表面车辆导航仿真任务中评估，并在四足机器人硬件上进行参考跟踪实验验证，MPPI-DK在保持接近真实动力学MPPI控制性能的同时，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: MPPI-DK框架通过深度Koopman算子实现了高效实时控制，在机器人平台上验证了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient model predictive path integral (MPPI) control framework for systems with complex nonlinear dynamics. To improve the computational efficiency of classic MPPI while preserving control performance, we replace the nonlinear dynamics used for trajectory propagation with a learned linear deep Koopman operator (DKO) model, enabling faster rollout and more efficient trajectory sampling. The DKO dynamics are learned directly from interaction data, eliminating the need for analytical system models. The resulting controller, termed MPPI-DK, is evaluated in simulation on pendulum balancing and surface vehicle navigation tasks, and validated on hardware through reference-tracking experiments on a quadruped robot. Experimental results demonstrate that MPPI-DK achieves control performance close to MPPI with true dynamics while substantially reducing computational cost, enabling efficient real-time control on robotic platforms.

</details>


### [40] [Distributed State Estimation for Vision-Based Cooperative Slung Load Transportation in GPS-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04571)
*Jack R. Pence,Jackson Fezell,Jack W. Langelaan,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉的分布式去中心化多旋翼协同吊运载荷状态估计框架，使用单目相机检测载荷标记，通过DDEIF融合估计，实现GPS拒止环境下的鲁棒控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单机吊运系统存在载荷容量和控制能力限制，现有协同吊运研究多依赖GPS或集中式架构，缺乏传感器失效鲁棒性，无法在GPS拒止或受限环境中工作。

Method: 使用机载单目相机检测载荷上的基准标记并估计相对位姿，通过分布式去中心化扩展信息滤波器（DDEIF）融合各无人机测量，实现鲁棒可扩展的状态估计，用于闭环轨迹跟踪控制。

Result: Gazebo中的蒙特卡洛仿真结果表明该方法有效，包括飞行中通信丢失情况下的性能验证。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式去中心化视觉框架解决了GPS拒止环境下多旋翼协同吊运的鲁棒状态估计问题，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transporting heavy or oversized slung loads using rotorcraft has traditionally relied on single-aircraft systems, which limits both payload capacity and control authority. Cooperative multilift using teams of rotorcraft offers a scalable and efficient alternative, especially for infrequent but challenging "long-tail" payloads without the need of building larger and larger rotorcraft. Most prior multilift research assumes GPS availability, uses centralized estimation architectures, or relies on controlled laboratory motion-capture setups. As a result, these methods lack robustness to sensor loss and are not viable in GPS-denied or operationally constrained environments.
